# Concept index — one subject, every channel, every area

**Start here when answering a subject question.** Channels file the same subject under different areas; the concept tags cut across them. Find the concept(s) that match the question in the table (labels and Russian/English synonyms), then open its section below: it lists **every topic of every channel** carrying that tag, with the channel, the area it is filed under and a one-line scope. Read each listed topic file in full (the channel's `agent-kb/SKILL.md` first). Then run the safety-net search with the concept's synonyms — `python3 tg-archivist/archivist.py find --concept <slug> "<the user's own words>"` — and read any relevant hit that is not tagged yet.

Source of truth: `concepts.json` (vocabulary) and each channel's `topics/_taxonomy.json` (the tags). Generated by `archivist.py sync` — do not edit. Machine twin: `index/concepts.json`.

168 concepts in use · 115 shared by several channels.
